+ "\n\nconstant constexpr int QCCE_THREADS = 128;\nconstant constexpr int QCCE_NUM_SGS = 4;\nconstant constexpr int QCCE_MAX_GROUPS = 64;  // upper bound on D / GS\n\ntemplate <typename T, int GS, int BM, int BITS>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_forward(\n    const device T*        x            [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device uint32_t* w_packed     [[buffer(1)]],\n    const device T*        scales       [[buffer(2)]],\n    const device T*        biases       [[buffer(3)]],\n    const device int32_t*  targets      [[buffer(4)]],\n    device float*          lse_out      [[buffer(5)]],\n    device float*          loss_per_row [[buffer(6)]],\n    const constant int&    M            [[buffer(7)]],\n    const constant int&    D            [[buffer(8)]],\n    const constant int&    V            [[buffer(9)]],\n    threadgroup T*         x_tile       [[threadgroup(0)]],\n    uint                   gid          [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint                   tid          [[thread_position_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint                   lane         [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],\n    uint                   sg_id        [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {\n  const int m_base = int(gid) * BM;\n  if (m_base >= M) return;\n\n  constexpr int EL_PER_PACK = 32 / BITS;       // 8 for bits=4, 4 for bits=8\n  const int num_groups   = D / GS;\n  const int packs_per_row = D / EL_PER_PACK;\n  const int packs_per_grp = GS / EL_PER_PACK;\n\n  threadgroup int   tg_target[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_target_logit[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_sg_max[BM][QCCE_NUM_SGS];\n  threadgroup float tg_sg_sum[BM][QCCE_NUM_SGS];\n  threadgroup float x_sum_tg[BM][QCCE_MAX_GROUPS];\n\n  // Cooperative load: x[m_base..m_base+BM, :] -> x_tile[BM*D]\n  for (int idx = int(tid); idx < BM * D; idx += QCCE_THREADS) {\n    const int r = idx / D;\n    const int k = idx - r * D;\n    x_tile[r * D + k] = x[(m_base + r) * D + k];\n  }\n  if (tid < BM) {\n    tg_target[tid] = targets[m_base + tid];\n    tg_target_logit[tid] = 0.0f;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Precompute x_sum[r][g] = sum over k in group g of x_tile[r, k].\n  // Each thread handles one (r, g) — only first BM*num_groups threads do work.\n  for (int idx = int(tid); idx < BM * num_groups; idx += QCCE_THREADS) {\n    const int r = idx / num_groups;\n    const int g = idx - r * num_groups;\n    float xs = 0.0f;\n    const int k_start = g * GS;\n    for (int k = 0; k < GS; k++) {\n      xs += float(x_tile[r * D + k_start + k]);\n    }\n    x_sum_tg[r][g] = xs;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Per-thread, per-row online LSE state — all threads track identical values\n  // (they consume the same TG-broadcast m_tile/s_tile per iteration).\n  float m_running[BM];\n  float s_running[BM];\n  for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n    m_running[r] = -INFINITY;\n    s_running[r] = 0.0f;\n  }\n\n  const int num_tiles = (V + QCCE_THREADS - 1) / QCCE_THREADS;\n  for (int tile = 0; tile < num_tiles; tile++) {\n    const int v = tile * QCCE_THREADS + int(tid);\n    float logit[BM];\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) logit[r] = -INFINITY;\n\n    if (v < V) {\n      for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) logit[r] = 0.0f;\n      const device uint32_t* w_row = w_packed + v * packs_per_row;\n      const device T* s_row_p = scales + v * num_groups;\n      const device T* b_row_p = biases + v * num_groups;\n\n      for (int g = 0; g < num_groups; g++) {\n        float s = float(s_row_p[g]);\n        float b = float(b_row_p[g]);\n        float qx_sum[BM];\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) qx_sum[r] = 0.0f;\n        const int k0_g = g * GS;\n        for (int p = 0; p < packs_per_grp; p++) {\n          const int k0 = k0_g + p * EL_PER_PACK;\n          uint32_t packed = w_row[k0 / EL_PER_PACK];  // shared across BM rows\n          float qs[EL_PER_PACK];\n          if constexpr (BITS == 4) {\n            for (int byte_idx = 0; byte_idx < 4; byte_idx++) {\n              uint8_t byte = (packed >> (byte_idx * 8)) & 0xff;\n              qs[byte_idx * 2]     = float(byte & 0x0f);\n              qs[byte_idx * 2 + 1] = float((byte >> 4) & 0x0f);\n            }\n          } else {  // BITS == 8\n            for (int byte_idx = 0; byte_idx < 4; byte_idx++) {\n              qs[byte_idx] = float((packed >> (byte_idx * 8)) & 0xff);\n            }\n          }\n          for (int i = 0; i < EL_PER_PACK; i++) {\n            for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n              qx_sum[r] += qs[i] * float(x_tile[r * D + k0 + i]);\n            }\n          }\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n          logit[r] += s * qx_sum[r] + b * x_sum_tg[r][g];\n        }\n      }\n\n      for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n        if (v == tg_target[r]) tg_target_logit[r] = logit[r];\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Per-row TG-wide max via 4-SG partial reduction.\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n      float sg_max = simd_max(logit[r]);\n      if (lane == 0) tg_sg_max[r][sg_id] = sg_max;\n    }\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n    float new_m[BM];\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n      float m_tile = max(max(tg_sg_max[r][0], tg_sg_max[r][1]),\n                         max(tg_sg_max[r][2], tg_sg_max[r][3]));\n      new_m[r] = max(m_running[r], m_tile);\n    }\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n      float exp_term = (logit[r] > -INFINITY) ? metal::fast::exp(logit[r] - new_m[r]) : 0.0f;\n      float sg_sum = simd_sum(exp_term);\n      if (lane == 0) tg_sg_sum[r][sg_id] = sg_sum;\n    }\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n      float s_tile = tg_sg_sum[r][0] + tg_sg_sum[r][1] + tg_sg_sum[r][2] + tg_sg_sum[r][3];\n      float scale_old = (m_running[r] > -INFINITY) ? metal::fast::exp(m_running[r] - new_m[r]) : 0.0f;\n      s_running[r] = s_running[r] * scale_old + s_tile;\n      m_running[r] = new_m[r];\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (tid < BM) {\n    float lse = metal::fast::log(s_running[tid]) + m_running[tid];\n    lse_out[m_base + tid] = lse;\n    loss_per_row[m_base + tid] = lse - tg_target_logit[tid];\n  }\n}\n\n// Mean reduction: loss = mean(loss_per_row). One TG, 32 lanes (M is small).\ntemplate <typename T>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_loss_reduce(\n    const device float* loss_per_row [[buffer(0)]],\n    device T*           loss_out     [[buffer(1)]],\n    const constant int& M            [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint                lane         [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]]) {\n  float sum = 0.0f;\n  for (int i = int(lane); i < M; i += 32) {\n    sum += loss_per_row[i];\n  }\n  sum = simd_sum(sum);\n  if (lane == 0) {\n    loss_out[0] = T(sum / float(M));\n  }\n}\n\n// Backward: d_x[m, k] = sum_v (softmax[m, v] * c_soft[m] - 1{v==target[m]} * c_one[m])\n//                       * W[v, k]\n//\n// c_soft and c_one are per-row and already include any 1/M scaling. They are separate\n// because the vjp of the vocab-tiled head (QuantizedCutCrossEntropyTileFwd::vjp) gives the\n// softmax and one-hot terms different weights, and this lets it run in one pass.\n//\n// Two-phase per V-tile (128 v's per tile):\n//   Phase 1 (tid = v offset): recompute logit per row, derive d_logit per row,\n//     store in d_logit_tile[BM][128].\n//   Phase 2 (tid = k_chunk index): for each of 128 v's in this tile, dequant 8\n//     W values once and FMA into accum[BM][8]; flush into d_x_acc[BM][D]. The\n//     per-TG fp32 accumulator is private to the TG.\n//\n// Grid: (M/BM, V/128, 1) — qmm-style tiling. Each TG handles BM rows × one\n// 128-v tile. Many small TGs keep all GPU cores busy. At end, the per-TG\n// d_x_acc[BM, D] is atomic_fetch_add'd into d_x_acc_global[M, D] fp32.\n// Caller must zero d_x_acc_global before dispatching this kernel.\ntemplate <typename T, int GS, int BM, int BITS>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_backward(\n    const device float*    c_soft         [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device T*        x              [[buffer(1)]],\n    const device uint32_t* w_packed       [[buffer(2)]],\n    const device T*        scales         [[buffer(3)]],\n    const device T*        biases         [[buffer(4)]],\n    const device int32_t*  targets        [[buffer(5)]],\n    const device float*    lse            [[buffer(6)]],\n    device atomic_float*   d_x_acc_global [[buffer(7)]],\n    const constant int&    M              [[buffer(8)]],\n    const constant int&    D              [[buffer(9)]],\n    const constant int&    V              [[buffer(10)]],\n    const device float*    c_one          [[buffer(11)]],\n    threadgroup T*         x_tile         [[threadgroup(0)]],\n    threadgroup float*     d_x_acc        [[threadgroup(1)]],\n    uint2                  gid            [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint2                  tid_v          [[thread_position_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint                   lane           [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],\n    uint                   sg_id          [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {\n  const uint tid = tid_v.x;\n  const int m_base = int(gid.x) * BM;\n  if (m_base >= M) return;\n  const int v_base = int(gid.y) * QCCE_THREADS;\n  if (v_base >= V) return;\n\n  constexpr int EL_PER_PACK = 32 / BITS;\n  const int num_groups   = D / GS;\n  const int packs_per_row = D / EL_PER_PACK;\n  const int packs_per_grp = GS / EL_PER_PACK;\n\n  threadgroup int   tg_target[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_lse[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_c_soft[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_c_one[BM];\n  threadgroup float d_logit_tile[BM][QCCE_THREADS];\n  threadgroup float x_sum_tg[BM][QCCE_MAX_GROUPS];\n\n  // Cooperative load: x rows -> x_tile, zero TG-local d_x_acc.\n  for (int idx = int(tid); idx < BM * D; idx += QCCE_THREADS) {\n    const int r = idx / D;\n    const int k = idx - r * D;\n    x_tile[r * D + k] = x[(m_base + r) * D + k];\n    d_x_acc[r * D + k] = 0.0f;\n  }\n  if (tid < BM) {\n    tg_target[tid] = targets[m_base + tid];\n    tg_lse[tid]    = lse[m_base + tid];\n    tg_c_soft[tid] = c_soft[m_base + tid];\n    tg_c_one[tid]  = c_one[m_base + tid];\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Precompute x_sum[r][g] = sum over k in group g of x_tile[r, k].\n  for (int idx = int(tid); idx < BM * num_groups; idx += QCCE_THREADS) {\n    const int r = idx / num_groups;\n    const int g = idx - r * num_groups;\n    float xs = 0.0f;\n    const int k_start = g * GS;\n    for (int k = 0; k < GS; k++) {\n      xs += float(x_tile[r * D + k_start + k]);\n    }\n    x_sum_tg[r][g] = xs;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Single 128-v tile per TG: tid = v offset within tile.\n  const int v = v_base + int(tid);\n\n  // Phase 1: recompute logit per row for this thread's v.\n  float logit[BM];\n  for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) logit[r] = -INFINITY;\n\n  if (v < V) {\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) logit[r] = 0.0f;\n    const device uint32_t* w_row = w_packed + v * packs_per_row;\n    const device T* s_row_p = scales + v * num_groups;\n    const device T* b_row_p = biases + v * num_groups;\n\n    for (int g = 0; g < num_groups; g++) {\n      float s = float(s_row_p[g]);\n      float b = float(b_row_p[g]);\n      float qx_sum[BM];\n      for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) qx_sum[r] = 0.0f;\n      const int k0_g = g * GS;\n      for (int p = 0; p < packs_per_grp; p++) {\n        const int k0 = k0_g + p * EL_PER_PACK;\n        uint32_t packed = w_row[k0 / EL_PER_PACK];\n        float qs[EL_PER_PACK];\n        if constexpr (BITS == 4) {\n          for (int byte_idx = 0; byte_idx < 4; byte_idx++) {\n            uint8_t byte = (packed >> (byte_idx * 8)) & 0xff;\n            qs[byte_idx * 2]     = float(byte & 0x0f);\n            qs[byte_idx * 2 + 1] = float((byte >> 4) & 0x0f);\n          }\n        } else {  // BITS == 8\n          for (int byte_idx = 0; byte_idx < 4; byte_idx++) {\n            qs[byte_idx] = float((packed >> (byte_idx * 8)) & 0xff);\n          }\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < EL_PER_PACK; i++) {\n          for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n            qx_sum[r] += qs[i] * float(x_tile[r * D + k0 + i]);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n        logit[r] += s * qx_sum[r] + b * x_sum_tg[r][g];\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n    float p_val = 0.0f;\n    if (v < V) {\n      p_val = metal::fast::exp(logit[r] - tg_lse[r]) * tg_c_soft[r];\n      if (v == tg_target[r]) p_val -= tg_c_one[r];\n    }\n    d_logit_tile[r][tid] = p_val;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Phase 2: each thread handles k_chunks, accumulates over all 128 v's of\n  // this tile. W reads are shared across BM rows.\n  for (int k_chunk = int(tid); k_chunk < packs_per_row; k_chunk += QCCE_THREADS) {\n    const int k0 = k_chunk * EL_PER_PACK;\n    const int g  = k0 / GS;\n    float accum[BM][EL_PER_PACK];\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n      for (int i = 0; i < EL_PER_PACK; i++) accum[r][i] = 0.0f;\n    }\n\n    for (int vi = 0; vi < QCCE_THREADS; vi++) {\n      const int v_global = v_base + vi;\n      if (v_global >= V) break;\n      uint32_t packed = w_packed[v_global * packs_per_row + k_chunk];\n      float s = float(scales[v_global * num_groups + g]);\n      float b = float(biases[v_global * num_groups + g]);\n      float qs[EL_PER_PACK];\n      if constexpr (BITS == 4) {\n        for (int byte_idx = 0; byte_idx < 4; byte_idx++) {\n          uint8_t byte = (packed >> (byte_idx * 8)) & 0xff;\n          qs[byte_idx * 2]     = float(byte & 0x0f);\n          qs[byte_idx * 2 + 1] = float((byte >> 4) & 0x0f);\n        }\n      } else {  // BITS == 8\n        for (int byte_idx = 0; byte_idx < 4; byte_idx++) {\n          qs[byte_idx] = float((packed >> (byte_idx * 8)) & 0xff);\n        }\n      }\n      for (int i = 0; i < EL_PER_PACK; i++) {\n        float w_i = s * qs[i] + b;\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n          float dl = d_logit_tile[r][vi];\n          accum[r][i] += dl * w_i;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < BM; r++) {\n      for (int i = 0; i < EL_PER_PACK; i++) {\n        d_x_acc[r * D + k0 + i] = accum[r][i];\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Atomic flush: add this TG's partial d_x to global. Each TG owns a unique\n  // (m_block, v_block) pair; multiple v_blocks contribute to same d_x[m, k],\n  // so atomic_fetch_add is required for cross-TG accumulation.\n  for (int idx = int(tid); idx < BM * D; idx += QCCE_THREADS) {\n    const int r = idx / D;\n    const int k = idx - r * D;\n    atomic_fetch_add_explicit(\n        &d_x_acc_global[(m_base + r) * D + k],\n        d_x_acc[r * D + k],\n        memory_order_relaxed);\n  }\n}\n\n// Zero a fp32 buffer. One thread per element. Caller dispatches with N total\n// threads to clear d_x_acc_global before the atomic-accumulating backward.\n[[kernel]] void qcce_zero_fp32(\n    device float*       buf [[buffer(0)]],\n    const constant int& N   [[buffer(1)]],\n    uint                gid [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {\n  if (int(gid) >= N) return;\n  buf[gid] = 0.0f;\n}\n\n// Cast the fp32 chunk-accumulated d_x into the final T-typed d_x output.\n// One thread per element; total threads = M * D.\ntemplate <typename T>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_backward_finalize(\n    const device float* d_x_acc_global [[buffer(0)]],\n    device T*           d_x            [[buffer(1)]],\n    const constant int& N              [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint                gid            [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {\n  if (int(gid) >= N) return;\n  d_x[gid] = T(d_x_acc_global[gid]);\n}\n\n"
+ "\n\ntemplate <\n    typename T,\n    int GS,\n    int BITS,\n    int BM,\n    int BN,\n    int BK,\n    int WM,\n    int WN,\n    bool aligned_M,\n    bool aligned_V>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_forward_steel(\n    const device T*        x            [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device uint32_t* w_packed     [[buffer(1)]],\n    const device T*        scales       [[buffer(2)]],\n    const device T*        biases       [[buffer(3)]],\n    const device int32_t*  targets      [[buffer(4)]],\n    device float*          partials     [[buffer(5)]],\n    const constant int&    M            [[buffer(6)]],\n    const constant int&    D            [[buffer(7)]],\n    const constant int&    V            [[buffer(8)]],\n    const constant int&    V_slab       [[buffer(9)]],\n    uint3                  tid          [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint                   lid          [[thread_index_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint                   sg_id        [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint                   lane         [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]]) {\n  constexpr int tgp_size = WM * WN * 32;\n  constexpr int BK_padded = BK + 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr int PACK = 32 / BITS;\n  constexpr int BPP = 4;  // bytes_per_pack: uint32 -> 4 bytes\n\n  threadgroup T     Xs[BM * BK_padded];\n  threadgroup T     Ws[BN * BK_padded];\n  threadgroup float logits_tile[BM * BN];\n  threadgroup float m_running[BM];\n  threadgroup float s_running[BM];\n  threadgroup float target_logit[BM];\n  threadgroup int   tg_target[BM];\n\n  const int m_base = int(tid.x) * BM;\n  if (m_base >= M) return;\n  const int m_actual = aligned_M ? BM : min(BM, M - m_base);\n  const int v_slab_start = int(tid.y) * V_slab;\n  if (v_slab_start >= V) return;\n  const int v_slab_end = min(v_slab_start + V_slab, V);\n\n  if (lid < uint(BM)) {\n    m_running[lid]     = -INFINITY;\n    s_running[lid]     = 0.0f;\n    target_logit[lid]  = 0.0f;\n    tg_target[lid]     = (int(lid) < m_actual) ? targets[m_base + lid] : -1;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  using mma_t = mlx::steel::BlockMMA<\n      T, T, BM, BN, BK, WM, WN, false, true, BK_padded, BK_padded>;\n  using loader_x_t = mlx::steel::BlockLoader<\n      T, BM, BK, BK_padded, 1, tgp_size>;\n  using loader_w_t = QuantizedBlockLoader<\n      T, BN, BK, BK_padded, 1, tgp_size, GS, BITS>;\n\n  const int K_w = D * BPP / PACK;\n  const int K_g = D / GS;\n\n  for (int v_tile = v_slab_start; v_tile < v_slab_end; v_tile += BN) {\n    const device T* x_ptr = x + m_base * (int64_t)D;\n    const device uint8_t* w_ptr =\n        ((const device uint8_t*)w_packed) + v_tile * (int64_t)K_w;\n    const device T* s_ptr = scales + v_tile * (int64_t)K_g;\n    const device T* b_ptr = biases + v_tile * (int64_t)K_g;\n\n    loader_x_t loader_x(x_ptr, D, Xs, sg_id, lane);\n    loader_w_t loader_w(w_ptr, s_ptr, b_ptr, D, Ws, sg_id, lane);\n    mma_t mma_op(sg_id, lane);\n    mma_op.Ctile.clear();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < D; k += BK) {\n      threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n      if (aligned_M) {\n        loader_x.load_unsafe();\n      } else {\n        loader_x.load_safe(short2(BK, m_actual));\n      }\n      loader_w.load_unsafe();\n      threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n      mma_op.mma(Xs, Ws);\n      loader_x.next();\n      loader_w.next();\n    }\n\n    // Ctile (fp32, BM×BN) -> logits_tile in TG memory, row-major.\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    mma_op.Ctile.template store<float, WM, WN, BN, 1>(\n        logits_tile + mma_op.sm * BN + mma_op.sn);\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n    // Per-row online-LSE reduction. BM rows split evenly across WM*WN SGs;\n    // each SG handles ROWS_PER_SG rows sequentially. Within a row, the SG's\n    // 32 lanes hold its BN=32 logits, reduced via simd_max / simd_sum.\n    constexpr int ROWS_PER_SG = BM / (WM * WN);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (int rr = 0; rr < ROWS_PER_SG; rr++) {\n      int row = int(sg_id) * ROWS_PER_SG + rr;\n      int v_global = v_tile + int(lane);\n      // Mask V-tail lanes to -INFINITY so they contribute nothing to max/sum.\n      float logit = (!aligned_V && v_global >= V)\n          ? -INFINITY\n          : logits_tile[row * BN + int(lane)];\n      if (v_global == tg_target[row]) {\n        target_logit[row] = logit;\n      }\n      float m_tile = simd_max(logit);\n      float m_new  = max(m_running[row], m_tile);\n      float e      = (logit > -INFINITY) ? metal::fast::exp(logit - m_new) : 0.0f;\n      float s_tile = simd_sum(e);\n      if (lane == 0) {\n        float scale_old = (m_running[row] > -INFINITY)\n            ? metal::fast::exp(m_running[row] - m_new)\n            : 0.0f;\n        s_running[row] = s_running[row] * scale_old + s_tile;\n        m_running[row] = m_new;\n      }\n    }\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n  }\n\n  // Emit partials[v_block, m_base+r, 0..2] for this (m_block, v_block).\n  if (lid < uint(BM)) {\n    int row = int(lid);\n    if (aligned_M || row < m_actual) {\n      int idx = (int(tid.y) * M + m_base + row) * 3;\n      partials[idx + 0] = m_running[row];\n      partials[idx + 1] = s_running[row];\n      partials[idx + 2] = target_logit[row];\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// Combine per-(v_block, row) partials into final lse[row] + loss_per_row[row].\n// Grid: ceil(M/TPG), TPG=128. Each thread owns one row and sequentially\n// log-add-exp-merges the grid_v slab partials with stable scaling. This is an\n// LSE monoid reduction — associative + commutative (identity -inf) — so slabs\n// merge in any order; the same property lets a caller split the vocab across\n// tiles and combine the per-tile lse downstream. target is a scalar per row so\n// at most one slab captured a non-zero target_logit; initial zero-init + sum\n// recovers it.\n[[kernel]] void qcce_reduce_partials(\n    const device float* partials     [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float*       lse_out      [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float*       loss_per_row [[buffer(2)]],\n    const constant int& M            [[buffer(3)]],\n    const constant int& grid_v       [[buffer(4)]],\n    uint                gid          [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {\n  const int row = int(gid);\n  if (row >= M) return;\n\n  float m_acc = -INFINITY;\n  float s_acc = 0.0f;\n  float tgt_acc = 0.0f;\n\n  for (int v = 0; v < grid_v; v++) {\n    int idx = (v * M + row) * 3;\n    float m_p   = partials[idx + 0];\n    float s_p   = partials[idx + 1];\n    float tgt_p = partials[idx + 2];\n\n    float m_new = max(m_acc, m_p);\n    float scale_old = (m_acc > -INFINITY)\n        ? metal::fast::exp(m_acc - m_new) : 0.0f;\n    float scale_p = (m_p > -INFINITY)\n        ? metal::fast::exp(m_p - m_new) : 0.0f;\n    s_acc = s_acc * scale_old + s_p * scale_p;\n    m_acc = m_new;\n    tgt_acc += tgt_p;\n  }\n\n  float lse = metal::fast::log(s_acc) + m_acc;\n  lse_out[row] = lse;\n  loss_per_row[row] = lse - tgt_acc;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Steel-based backward: hardware simdgroup_matrix FMA for both the Phase 1\n// logit recompute (X @ W^T) and the Phase 2 d_x accumulation (d_logit @ W).\n// Grid: (M/BM, V/V_slab). Each TG owns BM rows × one V-slab of V_slab v's,\n// walks the slab in BK-tile steps. For each v_tile:\n//   Phase 1: Steel MMA recomputes logits[BM, BN] (same kernel as forward).\n//   Phase 1b: convert logits to d_logit = exp(logit-lse) * c_soft[r]\n//             - (v==tgt) * c_one[r], stored as T in TG memory [BM, BN_padded].\n//             Like qcce_backward, the kernel does not divide by M, so the\n//             caller folds that scaling into c_soft and c_one.\n//   Phase 2: for each k_tile in D/BN, a fresh QuantizedBlockLoader with\n//            reduction_dim=0 loads W[v_tile:v_tile+BK, k_tile:k_tile+BN],\n//            one BlockMMA step (K-dim = BK = v-axis, fully reduced in one\n//            call) produces dx_partial[BM, BN]; atomic_fetch_add flushes\n//            into the global fp32 d_x_acc[M, D].\n// Post-kernel: qcce_backward_finalize<T> casts fp32 accumulator to T.\n//\n// Requires M % BM == 0 (handled by aligned_M template), D % BN == 0.\n// V need not be a multiple of BK — aligned_V templated bool. When false, the\n// last v_tile reads w_packed rows past V; safety:\n//   - Phase 1 W loader stays on load_unsafe (over-reads into garbage, but\n//     Phase 1b explicitly zeros d_logit for v_global >= V so garbage logits\n//     never reach the softmax/d_logit output). load_safe with reduction_dim=1\n//     would also no-op the row bound check per upstream MLX convention.\n//   - Phase 2 W loader uses load_safe (reduction_dim=0; correctly zeros rows\n//     past V — required because d_logit * NaN = NaN even with d_logit=0).\n//\n// Locked params: BM=BN=BK=32, WM=WN=2 -> tgp_size=128 (4 simdgroups, same\n// layout as forward). Phase 1 MMA reuses the forward instantiation exactly;\n// Phase 2 MMA is a second instantiation with (transpose_b=false) since W's\n// fast axis in that phase is the N-axis (D-axis), not the K-axis.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntemplate <\n    typename T,\n    int GS,\n    int BITS,\n    int BM,\n    int BN,\n    int BK,\n    int WM,\n    int WN,\n    bool aligned_M,\n    bool aligned_V>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_backward_steel(\n    const device float*    c_soft       [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device T*        x            [[buffer(1)]],\n    const device uint32_t* w_packed     [[buffer(2)]],\n    const device T*        scales       [[buffer(3)]],\n    const device T*        biases       [[buffer(4)]],\n    const device int32_t*  targets      [[buffer(5)]],\n    const device float*    lse          [[buffer(6)]],\n    device atomic_float*   d_x_acc      [[buffer(7)]],\n    const constant int&    M            [[buffer(8)]],\n    const constant int&    D            [[buffer(9)]],\n    const constant int&    V            [[buffer(10)]],\n    const constant int&    V_slab       [[buffer(11)]],\n    const device float*    c_one        [[buffer(12)]],\n    uint3                  tid          [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint                   lid          [[thread_index_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint                   sg_id        [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint                   lane         [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]]) {\n  constexpr int tgp_size   = WM * WN * 32;\n  constexpr int BK_padded  = BK + 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr int BN_padded  = BN + 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr int PACK       = 32 / BITS;\n  constexpr int BPP        = 4;\n\n  threadgroup T     Xs[BM * BK_padded];\n  threadgroup T     Ws_p1[BN * BK_padded];          // Phase 1: W[BN rows, BK cols]\n  threadgroup float logits_tile[BM * BN];\n  threadgroup T     d_logit_tile[BM * BN_padded];   // A matrix for Phase 2\n  threadgroup T     Ws_p2[BK * BN_padded];          // Phase 2: W[BK rows, BN cols]\n  threadgroup float dx_flush[BM * BN];\n  threadgroup float tg_lse[BM];\n  threadgroup int   tg_target[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_c_soft[BM];\n  threadgroup float tg_c_one[BM];\n\n  const int m_base = int(tid.x) * BM;\n  if (m_base >= M) return;\n  const int m_actual = aligned_M ? BM : min(BM, M - m_base);\n  const int v_slab_start = int(tid.y) * V_slab;\n  if (v_slab_start >= V) return;\n  const int v_slab_end = min(v_slab_start + V_slab, V);\n\n  if (lid < uint(BM)) {\n    bool in_range = aligned_M || int(lid) < m_actual;\n    tg_lse[lid]    = in_range ? lse[m_base + lid] : 0.0f;\n    tg_target[lid] = in_range ? targets[m_base + lid] : -1;\n    tg_c_soft[lid] = in_range ? c_soft[m_base + lid] : 0.0f;\n    tg_c_one[lid]  = in_range ? c_one[m_base + lid] : 0.0f;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Phase 1 MMA: logits[BM, BN] = X[BM, BK-loop] @ W[BN, BK-loop]^T\n  using mma_p1_t = mlx::steel::BlockMMA<\n      T, T, BM, BN, BK, WM, WN, false, true, BK_padded, BK_padded>;\n  using loader_x_t = mlx::steel::BlockLoader<\n      T, BM, BK, BK_padded, 1, tgp_size>;\n  using loader_w_p1_t = QuantizedBlockLoader<\n      T, BN, BK, BK_padded, 1, tgp_size, GS, BITS>;\n\n  // Phase 2 MMA: d_x[BM, BN] = d_logit[BM, BK] @ W[BK, BN]\n  // A tile = d_logit[BM, BN_padded] with K-dim = BK = BN (one-call reduction).\n  // B tile = Ws_p2[BK, BN_padded] with reduction_dim=0 (rows are v's).\n  using mma_p2_t = mlx::steel::BlockMMA<\n      T, T, BM, BN, BK, WM, WN, false, false, BN_padded, BN_padded>;\n  using loader_w_p2_t = QuantizedBlockLoader<\n      T, BK, BN, BN_padded, 0, tgp_size, GS, BITS>;\n\n  const int K_w = D * BPP / PACK;\n  const int K_g = D / GS;\n\n  for (int v_tile = v_slab_start; v_tile < v_slab_end; v_tile += BK) {\n    const int v_actual = aligned_V ? BK : min(BK, V - v_tile);\n    const bool is_v_tail = !aligned_V && (v_actual < BK);\n    // === Phase 1: Steel MMA recompute of logits_tile[BM, BN] ===\n    const device T* x_ptr = x + m_base * (int64_t)D;\n    const device uint8_t* w_ptr_p1 =\n        ((const device uint8_t*)w_packed) + v_tile * (int64_t)K_w;\n    const device T* s_ptr_p1 = scales + v_tile * (int64_t)K_g;\n    const device T* b_ptr_p1 = biases + v_tile * (int64_t)K_g;\n\n    loader_x_t loader_x(x_ptr, D, Xs, sg_id, lane);\n    loader_w_p1_t loader_w_p1(w_ptr_p1, s_ptr_p1, b_ptr_p1, D, Ws_p1, sg_id, lane);\n    mma_p1_t mma_p1(sg_id, lane);\n    mma_p1.Ctile.clear();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < D; k += BK) {\n      threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n      if (aligned_M) {\n        loader_x.load_unsafe();\n      } else {\n        loader_x.load_safe(short2(BK, m_actual));\n      }\n      // reduction_dim=1 load_safe is a no-op for row bounds (upstream MLX\n      // convention) — Phase 1b masks d_logit for v_global >= V instead.\n      loader_w_p1.load_unsafe();\n      threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n      mma_p1.mma(Xs, Ws_p1);\n      loader_x.next();\n      loader_w_p1.next();\n    }\n\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    mma_p1.Ctile.template store<float, WM, WN, BN, 1>(\n        logits_tile + mma_p1.sm * BN + mma_p1.sn);\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n    // === Phase 1b: logits -> d_logit (as T, padded leading dim) ===\n    for (int idx = int(lid); idx < BM * BN; idx += tgp_size) {\n      int r = idx / BN;\n      int c = idx - r * BN;\n      int v_global = v_tile + c;\n      float p_scaled;\n      if (!aligned_V && v_global >= V) {\n        p_scaled = 0.0f;\n      } else {\n        float logit = logits_tile[r * BN + c];\n        float p = metal::fast::exp(logit - tg_lse[r]) * tg_c_soft[r];\n        if (v_global == tg_target[r]) {\n          p -= tg_c_one[r];\n        }\n        p_scaled = p;\n      }\n      d_logit_tile[r * BN_padded + c] = T(p_scaled);\n    }\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n    // === Phase 2: per-k_tile MMA + atomic-flush into d_x_acc ===\n    for (int k_tile = 0; k < kelems; k++) {\n          if ((j * TN_stride + k) < dst_tile_dims.x) {\n            c_elems[k] = C[offset_c + k * fdc];\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Apply epilogue\n        STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n        for (short k = 0; k < kelems; k++) {\n          accum[k] = epilogue_op.apply(accum[k], c_elems[k]);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Store results from simdgroup_matrix results into device memory */\n  METAL_FUNC void store_result(\n      device U* D,\n      const int ldd,\n      const device U* C,\n      const int ldc,\n      const int fdc,\n      thread const Epilogue& epilogue_op) const {\n    // Adjust for simdgroup and thread location\n    C += (sm)*ldc + (sn)*fdc;\n    D += (sm)*ldd + sn;\n\n    constexpr short kelems = decltype(Ctile)::kElemsPerFrag;\n\n    // Loop over all simdgroup tiles\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short i = 0; d < D; d += 32) {\n    acc += float(O[o_off + d]) * float(dO[do_off + d]);\n  }\n  acc = simd_sum(acc);\n\n  if (simd_lane_id == 0) {\n    Delta[(size_t(b) * params->Hq + h) * size_t(params->qL) + i] = acc;\n  }\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Shared ops + function constants for the GEMM backward kernels\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\nconstant bool bwd_align_Q [[function_constant(200)]];\nconstant bool bwd_align_K [[function_constant(201)]];\nconstant bool bwd_do_causal [[function_constant(301)]];\n\nstruct BwdSubOp {\n  template <typename T>\n  METAL_FUNC static constexpr T apply(T x, T y) {\n    return x - y;\n  }\n};\nstruct BwdExpSubOp { // exp2(x - y)\n  template <typename T>\n  METAL_FUNC static constexpr T apply(T x, T y) {\n    return fast::exp2(x - y);\n  }\n};\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Stage 4: dQ kernel.   dQ_i = scale * sum_j dS_ij * k_j\n//\n// Queries are tile rows, so the per-row L / Delta apply directly via row ops\n// (no transpose needed). device T* Q [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device T* K [[buffer(1)]],\n    const device T* V [[buffer(2)]],\n    const device T* dO [[buffer(3)]],\n    const device float* L [[buffer(4)]],\n    const device float* Delta [[buffer(5)]],\n    device T* dQ [[buffer(6)]],\n    const constant AttnVJPParams* params [[buffer(7)]],\n    uint simd_lane_id [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],\n    uint simd_group_id [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint3 tid [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint3 lid [[thread_position_in_threadgroup]]) { // clang-format on\n  (void)lid;\n\n  ulong3 tidl{tid.x, tid.y, tid.z};\n  const ulong kv_head = ulong(tid.y) / params->gqa_factor;\n\n  Q += tidl.z * params->Q_strides[0] + tidl.y * params->Q_strides[1] +\n      tidl.x * BQ * params->Q_strides[2];\n  dO += tidl.z * params->dO_strides[0] + tidl.y * params->dO_strides[1] +\n      tidl.x * BQ * params->dO_strides[2];\n  dQ += tidl.z * params->dQ_strides[0] + tidl.y * params->dQ_strides[1] +\n      tidl.x * BQ * params->dQ_strides[2];\n  K += tidl.z * params->K_strides[0] + kv_head * params->K_strides[1];\n  V += tidl.z * params->V_strides[0] + kv_head * params->V_strides[1];\n  L += (tidl.z * params->Hq + tidl.y) * ulong(params->qL) + tidl.x * BQ;\n  Delta += (tidl.z * params->Hq + tidl.y) * ulong(params->qL) + tidl.x * BQ;\n\n  constexpr short padQ = 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr short padK = 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr short padV = 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr short LDQ_tgp = BD + padQ;\n  constexpr short LDK_tgp = BK + padK;\n  constexpr short LDV_tgp = BD + padV;\n\n  threadgroup T Qs[BQ * LDQ_tgp];\n  threadgroup T dOs[BQ * LDQ_tgp];\n  // KV scratch reused across the three per-kb loads (K-transposed, V-transposed,\n  // K-normal). Sized for the largest of the layouts.\n  constexpr short tgp_kt = LDK_tgp * BD; // K loaded transposed -> [BD, BK]\n  constexpr short tgp_kn = BK * LDV_tgp; // K loaded normal     -> [BK, BD]\n  constexpr short tgp_kv = tgp_kt > tgp_kn ? tgp_kt : tgp_kn;\n  threadgroup T KVs[tgp_kv];\n\n  using QBlockLoader = BlockLoaderT<T, BQ, BD, LDQ_tgp, 1, 1, WM * WN * 32>;\n  using KTBlockLoader = BlockLoaderT<T, BK, BD, 1, LDK_tgp, 0, WM * WN * 32>;\n  using KNBlockLoader = BlockLoaderT<T, BK, BD, LDV_tgp, 1, 0, WM * WN * 32>;\n\n  QBlockLoader loader_q(Q, params->Q_strides[2], Qs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n  QBlockLoader loader_do(\n      dO, params->dO_strides[2], dOs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n\n  const AccumType scale_log2 = params->scale * M_LOG2E_F;\n\n  constexpr short kFragSize = 8;\n  using MMAFrag_acc_t = BaseMMAFrag<AccumType, kFragSize, kFragSize>;\n  constexpr int kNWarps = WM * WN;\n  constexpr int TQ = BQ / (kNWarps * kFragSize);\n  constexpr int TK = BK / kFragSize;\n  constexpr int TD = BD / kFragSize;\n  static_assert(TQ == 1, \"Check TQ\");\n\n  MMATile<AccumType, TQ, 1, MMAFrag_acc_t> Qtile;\n  MMATile<AccumType, 1, TK, MMAFrag_acc_t> Ktile;\n  MMATile<AccumType, TQ, TK, MMAFrag_acc_t> Stile; // holds P, then dS\n  MMATile<AccumType, TQ, TK, MMAFrag_acc_t> dPtile;\n  MMATile<AccumType, 1, 1, MMAFrag_acc_t> Vtile;\n  MMATile<AccumType, TQ, TD, MMAFrag_acc_t> dQtile;\n  dQtile.clear();\n\n  const short2 simd_coord = MMAFrag_acc_t::get_coord(simd_lane_id);\n  const short sm = simd_coord.y;\n  const short sn = simd_coord.x;\n  const short tm = kFragSize * TQ * simd_group_id;\n\n  const short Qs_offset = (tm + sm) * LDQ_tgp + sn;\n  const short Ks_offset = sm * LDK_tgp + sn;\n  const short Vs_offset = sm * LDV_tgp + sn;\n  constexpr short Qs_tile_stride = kFragSize;\n  constexpr short Ks_tile_stride = kFragSize * LDK_tgp;\n\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Load Q and dO blocks (fixed for this threadgroup).\n  if (!bwd_align_Q && int(tid.x) == params->NQ_aligned) {\n    loader_q.load_safe(short2(BD, params->qL_rem));\n    loader_do.load_safe(short2(BD, params->qL_rem));\n  } else {\n    loader_q.load_unsafe();\n    loader_do.load_unsafe();\n  }\n\n  // Per-row L and Delta (one row per thread, kRowsPerThread == 1).\n  constexpr short kRowsPT = decltype(Stile)::kRowsPerThread;\n  AccumType Lrow[kRowsPT];\n  AccumType Drow[kRowsPT];\n  STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n  for (short i = 0; i < kRowsPT; ++i) {\n    const int r = tm + sm + i * kFragSize;\n    const bool ok = (bwd_align_Q || int(tid.x) != params->NQ_aligned)\n        ? (r < BQ)\n        : (r < params->qL_rem);\n    Lrow[i] = ok ? L[r] : AccumType(0);\n    Drow[i] = ok ? Delta[r] : AccumType(0);\n  }\n\n  int kb_lim = params->NK;\n  if (bwd_do_causal) {\n    int q_max = (int(tid.x) + 1) * BQ + params->qL_off;\n    kb_lim = min(params->NK, (q_max + BK - 1) / BK);\n  }\n\n  for (int kb = 0; kb < kb_lim; kb++) {\n    const device T* Kb = K + kb * BK * params->K_strides[2];\n    const device T* Vb = V + kb * BK * params->V_strides[2];\n    const bool last_k = (!bwd_align_K && kb == params->NK_aligned);\n\n    // ----- S = Q @ Kᵀ  (K loaded transposed into KVs) -----\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    KTBlockLoader loader_kt(\n        Kb, params->K_strides[2], KVs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    if (last_k) {\n      loader_kt.load_safe(short2(BD, params->kL_rem));\n    } else {\n      loader_kt.load_unsafe();\n    }\n    Stile.clear();\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short dd = 0; dd < TD; dd++) {\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      Qtile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDQ_tgp, 1>(&Qs[Qs_offset + dd * Qs_tile_stride]);\n      Ktile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDK_tgp, 1>(&KVs[Ks_offset + dd * Ks_tile_stride]);\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      tile_matmad(Stile, Qtile, Ktile, Stile);\n    }\n\n    // Scale to base-2 units.\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short ii = 0; ii < decltype(Stile)::kElemsPerTile; ii++) {\n      Stile.elems()[ii] *= scale_log2;\n    }\n\n    // Length + causal masking -> -inf so P == 0 there.\n    using stile_t = decltype(Stile);\n    constexpr auto neg_inf = Limits<AccumType>::finite_min;\n    if (last_k) {\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short i = 0; i < stile_t::kTileRows; i++) {\n        STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n        for (short j = 0; j < stile_t::kTileCols; j++) {\n          short col = sn + j * stile_t::kFragCols;\n          STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n          for (short jj = 0; jj < stile_t::MMAFrag_t::kElemCols; jj++) {\n            if ((col + jj) >= params->kL_rem)\n              Stile.frag_at(i, j)[jj] = neg_inf;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (bwd_do_causal) {\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short i = 0; i < stile_t::kTileRows; i++) {\n        const int row = int(tid.x) * BQ + params->qL_off + tm + sm +\n            i * stile_t::kFragRows;\n        STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n        for (short j = 0; j < stile_t::kTileCols; j++) {\n          const int col = kb * BK + sn + j * stile_t::kFragCols;\n          STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n          for (short jj = 0; jj < stile_t::MMAFrag_t::kElemCols; jj++) {\n            if (row < (col + jj))\n              Stile.frag_at(i, j)[jj] = neg_inf;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // P = exp2(scaledS - L)\n    Stile.template row_bin_op<BwdExpSubOp>(Lrow);\n\n    // ----- dP = dO @ Vᵀ  (V loaded transposed) -----\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    KTBlockLoader loader_vt(\n        Vb, params->V_strides[2], KVs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    if (last_k) {\n      loader_vt.load_safe(short2(BD, params->kL_rem));\n    } else {\n      loader_vt.load_unsafe();\n    }\n    dPtile.clear();\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short dd = 0; dd < TD; dd++) {\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      Qtile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDQ_tgp, 1>(&dOs[Qs_offset + dd * Qs_tile_stride]);\n      Ktile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDK_tgp, 1>(&KVs[Ks_offset + dd * Ks_tile_stride]);\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      tile_matmad(dPtile, Qtile, Ktile, dPtile);\n    }\n\n    // dS = P ∘ (dP - Delta) * scale   (natural scale)\n    dPtile.template row_bin_op<BwdSubOp>(Drow);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short ii = 0; ii < decltype(Stile)::kElemsPerTile; ii++) {\n      Stile.elems()[ii] =\n          Stile.elems()[ii] * dPtile.elems()[ii] * params->scale;\n    }\n\n    // ----- dQ += dS @ K   (K loaded normal [BK,BD]) -----\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    KNBlockLoader loader_kn(\n        Kb, params->K_strides[2], KVs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    if (last_k) {\n      loader_kn.load_safe(short2(BD, params->kL_rem));\n    } else {\n      loader_kn.load_unsafe();\n    }\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short iq = 0; iq < TQ; iq++) {\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short id = 0; id < TD; id++) {\n        STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n        for (short ik = 0; ik < TK; ik++) {\n          if (BD == 128)\n            simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n          const short kk = ik * kFragSize;\n          const short dd = id * kFragSize;\n          Vtile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDV_tgp, 1>(&KVs[Vs_offset + kk * LDV_tgp + dd]);\n          if (BD == 128)\n            simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n          MMAFrag_acc_t::mma(\n              dQtile.frag_at(iq, id),\n              Stile.frag_at(iq, ik),\n              Vtile.frag_at(0, 0),\n              dQtile.frag_at(iq, id));\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Store dQ.\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n  dQ += (tm + sm) * params->dQ_strides[2] + sn;\n  if (!bwd_align_Q && int(tid.x) == params->NQ_aligned) {\n    auto dims = short2(BD - sn, params->qL_rem - (tm + sm));\n    if (dims.x <= 0 || dims.y <= 0)\n      return;\n    dQtile.template store_safe<T, 1, 1>(dQ, params->dQ_strides[2], dims);\n  } else {\n    dQtile.template store<T, 1, 1>(dQ, params->dQ_strides[2]);\n  }\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Stage 3: dK/dV kernel.   dV_j = sum_i P_ij dO_i ;  dK_j = scale sum_i dS_ij q_i\n//\n// P and dS are computed with QUERIES as rows (so per-row L/Delta apply), then\n// staged to threadgroup memory and re-loaded TRANSPOSED so KEYS become the\n// output rows for the dV/dK accumulation. One threadgroup per (batch,\n// query-head, key-block); the inner loop covers all query blocks, summing into\n// the persistent dK/dV register tiles -- which hold per-query-head PARTIALS,\n// summed across each gqa group by the reduce kernel below. Uses BQ==BK==32 so\n// both the [BQ,BK] (query-row split) and [BK,BD] (key-row split) tilings give\n// each of WM warps a full 8-row fragment.\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n// clang-format off\ntemplate <\n    typename T,\n    int BQ,\n    int BK,\n    int BD,\n    int WM,\n    int WN,\n    typename AccumType = float>\n[[kernel, max_total_threads_per_threadgroup(WM* WN * 32)]] void sdpa_vjp_dkdv(\n    const device T* Q [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device T* K [[buffer(1)]],\n    const device T* V [[buffer(2)]],\n    const device T* dO [[buffer(3)]],\n    const device float* L [[buffer(4)]],\n    const device float* Delta [[buffer(5)]],\n    device T* dK [[buffer(6)]],\n    device T* dV [[buffer(7)]],\n    const constant AttnVJPParams* params [[buffer(8)]],\n    uint simd_lane_id [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],\n    uint simd_group_id [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint3 tid [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint3 lid [[thread_position_in_threadgroup]]) { // clang-format on\n  (void)lid;\n\n  // Grid y is the QUERY head (not the kv head): one threadgroup per\n  // (key-block, query-head, batch), so no threadgroup loops over the gqa group.\n  // Each writes a per-query-head PARTIAL dK/dV into a contiguous\n  // [B, Hq, kL, D] scratch buffer, which sdpa_vjp_dkdv_reduce sums into the\n  // real [B, Hkv, kL, D] dK/dV. dK/dV here point at the partial buffer.\n  const int qh = int(tid.y); // query head\n  const int kvh = qh / params->gqa_factor; // kv head it maps to\n  const int b = int(tid.z);\n  const int kb = int(tid.x); // key block\n\n  // Partial output is contiguous [B, Hq, kL, D] (one plane per query head).\n  const int64_t p_strideB = int64_t(params->Hq) * params->kL * params->D;\n  const int64_t p_strideH = int64_t(params->kL) * params->D;\n  const int64_t p_strideRow = params->D;\n\n  K += b * params->K_strides[0] + kvh * params->K_strides[1] +\n      kb * BK * params->K_strides[2];\n  V += b * params->V_strides[0] + kvh * params->V_strides[1] +\n      kb * BK * params->V_strides[2];\n  dK += b * p_strideB + qh * p_strideH + kb * BK * p_strideRow;\n  dV += b * p_strideB + qh * p_strideH + kb * BK * p_strideRow;\n\n  constexpr short padA = 16 / sizeof(T);\n  constexpr short padS = 16 / sizeof(float);\n  constexpr short LDX_tgp = BD + padA; // for [*, BD] tiles\n  constexpr short LDS_tgp = BK + padS; // for [BQ, BK] staged P/dS (float)\n  constexpr short LDKT_tgp = BK + padA; // K/V transposed -> [BD, BK]\n\n  threadgroup T Qs[BQ * LDX_tgp];\n  threadgroup T dOs[BQ * LDX_tgp];\n  // Scratch reused in two disjoint phases:\n  //  phase A (build S/dP): K-transposed then V-transposed -> [BD, BK] in T\n  //  phase B (accumulate): staged P and dS -> [BQ, BK] each, in float so\n  //  neither is rounded to T before the dV/dK products\n  // Sized in floats to cover whichever phase needs more bytes.\n  constexpr int tgp_phaseA_bytes = LDKT_tgp * BD * int(sizeof(T));\n  constexpr int tgp_phaseB_bytes = 2 * BQ * LDS_tgp * int(sizeof(float));\n  constexpr int tgp_scratch_bytes = tgp_phaseA_bytes > tgp_phaseB_bytes\n      ? tgp_phaseA_bytes\n      : tgp_phaseB_bytes;\n  threadgroup float scratch[(tgp_scratch_bytes + 3) / 4];\n  threadgroup T* KTs = reinterpret_cast<threadgroup T*>(scratch); // phase A\n  threadgroup float* Ps = scratch; // phase B\n  threadgroup float* dSs = scratch + (BQ * LDS_tgp); // phase B\n\n  using XBlockLoader = BlockLoaderT<T, BQ, BD, LDX_tgp, 1, 1, WM * WN * 32>;\n  using KTBlockLoader = BlockLoaderT<T, BK, BD, 1, LDKT_tgp, 0, WM * WN * 32>;\n\n  const AccumType scale_log2 = params->scale * M_LOG2E_F;\n\n  constexpr short kFragSize = 8;\n  using MMAFrag_acc_t = BaseMMAFrag<AccumType, kFragSize, kFragSize>;\n  constexpr int kNWarps = WM * WN;\n  constexpr int TQ = BQ / (kNWarps * kFragSize); // query-row tiles (==1)\n  constexpr int TKr = BK / (kNWarps * kFragSize); // key-row tiles    (==1)\n  constexpr int TK = BK / kFragSize; // key cols of S\n  constexpr int TQc = BQ / kFragSize; // query cols when transposed\n  constexpr int TD = BD / kFragSize;\n  static_assert(TQ == 1 && TKr == 1, \"Backward dKdV expects BQ==BK==WM*8\");\n\n  const short2 sc = MMAFrag_acc_t::get_coord(simd_lane_id);\n  const short sm = sc.y;\n  const short sn = sc.x;\n  // query-row warp split (phase A); key-row warp split (phase B)\n  const short tmq = kFragSize * TQ * simd_group_id;\n  const short tmk = kFragSize * TKr * simd_group_id;\n\n  // Persistent accumulators over key rows.\n  MMATile<AccumType, TKr, TD, MMAFrag_acc_t> dKtile;\n  MMATile<AccumType, TKr, TD, MMAFrag_acc_t> dVtile;\n  dKtile.clear();\n  dVtile.clear();\n\n  const bool last_k = (!bwd_align_K && kb == params->NK_aligned);\n\n  // K and V are both reloaded (transposed) from device on every query block\n  // rather than kept resident: `scratch` is reused for K, then V, then the\n  // staged P/dS, which bounds threadgroup memory at the cost of the reloads.\n\n  // This threadgroup handles exactly one query head (qh, from grid y). The\n  // gqa reduction across query heads is done by the separate reduce kernel.\n  const device T* Qh = Q + b * params->Q_strides[0] + qh * params->Q_strides[1];\n  const device T* dOh =\n      dO + b * params->dO_strides[0] + qh * params->dO_strides[1];\n  const device float* Lh =\n      L + (b * params->Hq + qh) * ulong(params->qL);\n  const device float* Dh =\n      Delta + (b * params->Hq + qh) * ulong(params->qL);\n\n  // For causal, only query blocks whose rows can attend to this key block.\n  int qb_start = 0;\n  if (bwd_do_causal) {\n    // key positions covered: [kb*BK, kb*BK+BK); a query at position p attends\n    // key c iff p+qL_off >= c. Earliest query block that can reach kb*BK.\n    int qpos_min = kb * BK - params->qL_off;\n    qb_start = qpos_min <= 0 ? 0 : (qpos_min / BQ);\n  }\n\n  for (int qb = qb_start; qb < params->NQ; ++qb) {\n    const bool last_q = (!bwd_align_Q && qb == params->NQ_aligned);\n    const device T* Qblk = Qh + qb * BQ * params->Q_strides[2];\n    const device T* dOblk = dOh + qb * BQ * params->dO_strides[2];\n\n    // ---- Load Q, dO blocks ----\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    XBlockLoader lq(Qblk, params->Q_strides[2], Qs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    XBlockLoader ld(dOblk, params->dO_strides[2], dOs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    if (last_q) {\n      lq.load_safe(short2(BD, params->qL_rem));\n      ld.load_safe(short2(BD, params->qL_rem));\n    } else {\n      lq.load_unsafe();\n      ld.load_unsafe();\n    }\n\n    // Per-(query)row L/Delta for phase A (query-row split tmq).\n    AccumType Lrow[TQ];\n    AccumType Drow[TQ];\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short i = 0; i < TQ; ++i) {\n      const int r = tmq + sm + i * kFragSize;\n      const bool ok =\n          last_q ? (r < params->qL_rem) : (r < BQ);\n      Lrow[i] = ok ? Lh[qb * BQ + r] : AccumType(0);\n      Drow[i] = ok ? Dh[qb * BQ + r] : AccumType(0);\n    }\n\n    // ---- S = Q @ Kᵀ ; P = exp2(scale*log2e*S - L) ----\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    KTBlockLoader lkt(K, params->K_strides[2], KTs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    if (last_k) {\n      lkt.load_safe(short2(BD, params->kL_rem));\n    } else {\n      lkt.load_unsafe();\n    }\n    MMATile<AccumType, TQ, 1, MMAFrag_acc_t> Atile;\n    MMATile<AccumType, 1, TK, MMAFrag_acc_t> Btile;\n    MMATile<AccumType, TQ, TK, MMAFrag_acc_t> Stile;\n    Stile.clear();\n    const short Qs_off = (tmq + sm) * LDX_tgp + sn;\n    const short Ks_off = sm * LDKT_tgp + sn;\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short dd = 0; dd < TD; dd++) {\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      Atile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDX_tgp, 1>(&Qs[Qs_off + dd * kFragSize]);\n      Btile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDKT_tgp, 1>(&KTs[Ks_off + dd * kFragSize * LDKT_tgp]);\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      tile_matmad(Stile, Atile, Btile, Stile);\n    }\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short ii = 0; ii < decltype(Stile)::kElemsPerTile; ii++)\n      Stile.elems()[ii] *= scale_log2;\n\n    using stile_t = decltype(Stile);\n    constexpr auto neg_inf = Limits<AccumType>::finite_min;\n    if (last_k) {\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short i = 0; i < stile_t::kTileRows; i++) {\n        STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n        for (short j = 0; j < stile_t::kTileCols; j++) {\n          short col = sn + j * stile_t::kFragCols;\n          STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n          for (short jj = 0; jj < stile_t::MMAFrag_t::kElemCols; jj++) {\n            if ((col + jj) >= params->kL_rem)\n              Stile.frag_at(i, j)[jj] = neg_inf;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (bwd_do_causal) {\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short i = 0; i < stile_t::kTileRows; i++) {\n        const int row = qb * BQ + params->qL_off + tmq + sm + i * stile_t::kFragRows;\n        STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n        for (short j = 0; j < stile_t::kTileCols; j++) {\n          const int col = kb * BK + sn + j * stile_t::kFragCols;\n          STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n          for (short jj = 0; jj < stile_t::MMAFrag_t::kElemCols; jj++)\n            if (row < (col + jj))\n              Stile.frag_at(i, j)[jj] = neg_inf;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    Stile.template row_bin_op<BwdExpSubOp>(Lrow); // P\n\n    // ---- dP = dO @ Vᵀ ; dS = P ∘ (dP - Delta), unscaled ----\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    KTBlockLoader lvt(V, params->V_strides[2], KTs, simd_group_id, simd_lane_id);\n    if (last_k) {\n      lvt.load_safe(short2(BD, params->kL_rem));\n    } else {\n      lvt.load_unsafe();\n    }\n    MMATile<AccumType, TQ, TK, MMAFrag_acc_t> dPtile;\n    dPtile.clear();\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short dd = 0; dd < TD; dd++) {\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      Atile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDX_tgp, 1>(&dOs[Qs_off + dd * kFragSize]);\n      Btile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDKT_tgp, 1>(&KTs[Ks_off + dd * kFragSize * LDKT_tgp]);\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      tile_matmad(dPtile, Atile, Btile, dPtile);\n    }\n    dPtile.template row_bin_op<BwdSubOp>(Drow);\n    // scale is applied to dK once after the loop rather than per element\n    // here; j < TK; j++) {\n        MMAFrag_acc_t::store(\n            Stile.frag_at(0, j), &Ps[Ps_off + j * kFragSize], Int<LDS_tgp>{}, Int<1>{});\n        MMAFrag_acc_t::store(\n            dStile.frag_at(0, j), &dSs[Ps_off + j * kFragSize], Int<LDS_tgp>{}, Int<1>{});\n      }\n    }\n    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n    // dV[BK,BD] += Pᵀ @ dO ; Contraction over BQ (queries).\n    MMATile<AccumType, TKr, 1, MMAFrag_acc_t> PTtile; // [key, query-frag]\n    MMATile<AccumType, TKr, 1, MMAFrag_acc_t> dSTtile;\n    MMATile<AccumType, 1, 1, MMAFrag_acc_t> Xtile; // single dO / Q frag\n    STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n    for (short ic = 0; ic < TQc; ic++) {\n      simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n      // load Pᵀ[key, ic] and dSᵀ[key, ic] from staged smem (transposed)\n      PTtile.template load<float, 1, 1, 1, LDS_tgp>(&Ps[(tmk + sm) + (ic * kFragSize) * LDS_tgp + sn * LDS_tgp]);\n      dSTtile.template load<float, 1, 1, 1, LDS_tgp>(&dSs[(tmk + sm) + (ic * kFragSize) * LDS_tgp + sn * LDS_tgp]);\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short id = 0; id < TD; id++) {\n        Xtile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDX_tgp, 1>(&dOs[(ic * kFragSize) * LDX_tgp + sm * LDX_tgp + sn + id * kFragSize]);\n        MMAFrag_acc_t::mma(\n            dVtile.frag_at(0, id), PTtile.frag_at(0, 0), Xtile.frag_at(0, 0), dVtile.frag_at(0, id));\n      }\n      STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n      for (short id = 0; id < TD; id++) {\n        Xtile.template load<T, 1, 1, LDX_tgp, 1>(&Qs[(ic * kFragSize) * LDX_tgp + sm * LDX_tgp + sn + id * kFragSize]);\n        MMAFrag_acc_t::mma(\n            dKtile.frag_at(0, id), dSTtile.frag_at(0, 0), Xtile.frag_at(0, 0), dKtile.frag_at(0, id));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // dK_j = scale * sum_i dS_ij q_i -- the dS staged above was unscaled.\n  STEEL_PRAGMA_UNROLL\n  for (short ii = 0; ii < decltype(dKtile)::kElemsPerTile; ii++)\n    dKtile.elems()[ii] *= params->scale;\n\n  // Store dK, dV (key rows) into the per-query-head partial buffer (row stride\n  // is the contiguous partial layout's D, not the real output's dK_strides).\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_none);\n  const short krow = tmk + sm;\n  dK += krow * p_strideRow + sn;\n  dV += krow * p_strideRow + sn;\n  if (last_k) {\n    auto dims = short2(BD - sn, params->kL_rem - krow);\n    if (dims.x > 0 && dims.y > 0) {\n      dKtile.template store_safe<T, 1, 1>(dK, p_strideRow, dims);\n      dVtile.template store_safe<T, 1, 1>(dV, p_strideRow, dims);\n    }\n  } else {\n    dKtile.template store<T, 1, 1>(dK, p_strideRow);\n    dVtile.template store<T, 1, 1>(dV, p_strideRow);\n  }\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// dK/dV GQA reduction.   dK[b,kvh,j,:] = sum_{g<gqa} dKp[b, kvh*gqa+g, j, :]\n//\n// The dkdv kernel above writes per-query-head partials into a contiguous\n// [B, Hq, kL, D] buffer (dKp/dVp). This sums each gqa group of query heads\n// down to the real [B, Hkv, kL, D] dK/dV. One threadgroup per (key-row j,\n// kv-head, batch); threads stride over the head dim, so any threadgroup width\n// is correct (the host dispatches width D: one element per thread).\n//\n// The sum is float32 but dKp/dVp are stored at q.dtype(), so partials are\n// already rounded to T before this reduction reads them. Measured at gqa=8:\n// that costs ~1.0x the rounding of dK/dV themselves, so it is at the noise\n// floor and widening the partials would not buy accuracy.\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n// clang-format off\ntemplate <typename T>\n[[kernel]] void sdpa_vjp_dkdv_reduce(\n    const device T* dKp [[buffer(0)]],\n    const device T* dVp [[buffer(1)]],\n    device T* dK [[buffer(2)]],\n    device T* dV [[buffer(3)]],\n    const constant AttnVJPParams* params [[buffer(4)]],\n    uint3 tid [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],\n    uint3 lid [[thread_position_in_threadgroup]],\n    uint3 tpg [[threads_per_threadgroup]]) { // clang-format on\n  const int j = int(tid.x); // key row\n  const int kvh = int(tid.y); // kv head\n  const int b = int(tid.z);\n  if (j >= params->kL) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const int gqa = params->gqa_factor;\n  const int D = params->D;\n  const int64_t p_strideB = int64_t(params->Hq) * params->kL * D;\n  const int64_t p_strideH = int64_t(params->kL) * D;\n\n  // Stride over the head dim rather than assume width == D: a wider dispatch\n  // leaves the extra threads idle, a narrower one gives each several elements.\n  for (int d = int(lid.x); d < D; d += int(tpg.x)) {\n    float accK = 0.0f;\n    float accV = 0.0f;\n    for (int g = 0; g < gqa; ++g) {\n      const int qh = kvh * gqa + g;\n      const int64_t off = b * p_strideB + qh * p_strideH + int64_t(j) * D + d;\n      accK += float(dKp[off]);\n      accV += float(dVp[off]);\n    }\n\n    const int64_t outK = b * params->dK_strides[0] +\n        kvh * params->dK_strides[1] + int64_t(j) * params->dK_strides[2] + d;\n    const int64_t outV = b * params->dV_strides[0] +\n        kvh * params->dV_strides[1] + int64_t(j) * params->dV_strides[2] + d;\n    dK[outK] = T(accK);\n    dV[outV] = T(accV);\n  }\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n"

k < GS; k++) {\n      xs += float(x_tile[r * D + k_start + k]);\n    }\n    x_sum_tg[r][g] = xs;\n  }\n  threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);\n\n  // Single 128-v tile per TG: tid = v offset within tile.\n  const int v = v_base + int(tid);\n\n  // Phase 1: recompute logit per row for this thread's v.\n  float logit[BM];\n  for (int r = 0; Caller dispatches with N total\n// threads to clear d_x_acc_global before the atomic-accumulating backward.\n[[kernel]] void qcce_zero_fp32(\n    device float*       buf [[buffer(0)]],\n    const constant int& N   [[buffer(1)]],\n    uint                gid [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {\n  if (int(gid) >= N) return;\n  buf[gid] = 0.0f;\n}\n\n// Cast the fp32 chunk-accumulated d_x into the final T-typed d_x output.\n// One thread per element; total threads = M * D.\ntemplate <typename T>\n[[kernel]] void qcce_backward_finalize(\n    const device float* d_x_acc_global [[buffer(0)]],\n    device T*           d_x            [[buffer(1)]],\n    const constant int& N              [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint                gid            [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {\n  if (int(gid) >= N) return;\n  d_x[gid] = T(d_x_acc_global[gid]);\n}\n\n"
